On click it passed the raw string to `new Entry(...)`, which does `new Base32().decode(secret.toUpperCase())` (Entry.java:90/102). commons-codec 1.14 throws an unchecked `IllegalArgumentException` for strings that are in the base32 alphabet but not a decodable value — `"test"` → `"TEST"` is 4 chars = 20 bits with non-zero discarded bits, so it throws from inside the `AlertDialog` button handler and kills the process. Unlike the QR/intent entry paths (`MainActivity:319`, `:850`) which already wrap `new Entry(...)` in `try/catch`, this path had no guard.\n\n## Changes\n\n**1. `Utilities/Tools.java` — new `isValidBase32(String)` helper**\n\nStrips whitespace, uppercases, rejects characters outside the base32 alphabet (they would otherwise be silently dropped, yielding a wrong secret), and catches `IllegalArgumentException` from a trial `decode()` instead of propagating it.\n\n**2. `Dialogs/ManualEntryDialog.java`**\n\n- The `TextWatcher` now validates the secret: invalid input sets an inline error on the secret field (`error_invalid_secret`) and keeps **Save** disabled — so the user gets the expected error message rather than a crash.\n- Safety net in the save handler: re-checks the secret and wraps entry creation in `try/catch (IllegalArgumentException)` with a toast, so no input can ever crash the dialog.\n- Replaced the bare `Integer.parseInt(...) == 0` / `Long.parseLong(...)` validation of digits/period/counter with `isPositiveNumber` / `isValidCounter` helpers — those parses threw `NumberFormatException` on overflow-length numeric input (e.g. a 15-digit period), a second latent crash on the same screen.\n\n**3. `res/values/strings_main.xml`** — added `error_invalid_secret` and `error_invalid_input`.\n\nI verified the validator against the actual commons-codec 1.14 jar: `test`/`TEST`/`A`/`1234`/`!!!!`/`""` → rejected; `JBSWY3DPEHPK3PXP`, lowercase, space-separated, padded (`MZXW6===`), unpadded, and short-but-valid (`AA`) secrets → accepted, so existing valid entries are unaffected.\n\nNote: this checkout isn\'t a git repo of its own (the enclosing research repo ignores it), so `git diff` produces no output — the edits are in the working tree at `app/src/main/java/.../Utilities/Tools.java`, `app/src/main/java/.../Dialogs/ManualEntryDialog.java`, and `app/src/main/res/values/strings_main.xml`.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 1376, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 51216, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 1376, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 3, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfAMEhTvwvd6F5ynBMPhZ', stop_reason=None, session_id='32d9e0f8-9736-4ee7-8e3f-a7b6275d2cd8', uuid='640019a9-c7ed-4ee6-8357-ae40325e30b0')
